# Findings

## enqor
- source_layer: Register
- owner: Align Networks Inc.
- owner_country: KR
- classes: 9
- status: PENDING
- dates: Filed 2026-04-14; Expiry —
- url: /mark/kr/a59ef335-9570-4757-8770-26b208dc24b1
- key_factors: The closest live conflict in the client's own named market. Align Networks holds three pending Korean applications — one in each of the client's three core classes (9, 41 and 42) — filed April 2026, ahead of any VENQORI filing. The official record specifies downloadable smartphone application software: the client's exact product on the client's exact channel. ENQOR sits bodily inside VENQORI, and a Korean app-store consumer decides from a truncated listing title at low attention. This changes the advice: Korean filing timing, whether to watch all three applications to publication for opposition, and whether to approach this owner for consent are all live decisions. A class 9 narrowing would not answer it, because the class 41 leg meets the client's training layer directly. Mitigants for the rating step: all three are pending and unexamined, and the V- onset and terminal -I are genuine points of difference.
- verify: yes
- resolution: adversarial / Moderate — see finding #1

## Ｖｅｎｋｕｒｉ
- source_layer: Register
- owner: YIWU TENGDING UMBRELLA CO., LTD.
- owner_country: CN
- classes: 9
- status: REGISTERED
- dates: Filed 2019-04-02; Expiry 2030-03-02
- url: /mark/jp/310027f2-0562-46fe-abea-385f068a8150
- key_factors: The nearest phonetic equivalent of the mark anywhere on the register, registered in class 9 in Japan — the client's other named market — to 2030. VEN-KU-RI against VEN-KO-RI is one medial vowel; in katakana, the form a Japanese examiner and a Japanese consumer actually compare, the two are close to indistinguishable. The class 9 specification is unrestricted on its face, so it reads across downloadable software whatever the owner actually sells. This is the record most likely to produce a citation against a Japanese application, so it decides whether the client files Japan as drafted, narrows the class 9 wording, or clears the path first. Substantial mitigant: the owner is an umbrella manufacturer with no visible software use and the mark has been registered since March 2020, so the Japanese three-year non-use window is wide open and cancellation is a realistic route that should be priced as one. This record was invisible to the mechanical similarity ranking — it is held in full-width characters — and was reached only through the phonetic search.
- verify: yes
- resolution: adversarial / Manageable — see finding #3

## VENKURI
- source_layer: Register
- owner: Yiwu Tengding Umbrella Co., Ltd.
- owner_country: CN
- classes: 9
- status: REGISTERED
- dates: Filed 2019-04-01; Expiry 2030-01-27
- url: /mark/cn/817a4fcd-a0f3-4048-b0de-2d9b598ca7f5
- key_factors: The Chinese half of the same owner's VENKURI holding, registered in class 9 to 2030. China is a reach market and a base from which this owner could designate Japan or Korea. Chinese examination applies a mechanical similarity test under which a seven-letter coined word one vowel away in the same class is a strong refusal risk, so a Chinese filing decision turns on this record. Same mitigant as the Japanese leg: an umbrella manufacturer with no apparent software use, and non-use cancellation available since January 2023.
- verify: yes
- resolution: adversarial / Manageable — see finding #3

## VENCORA
- source_layer: Register
- owner: Vencora UK Limited
- owner_country: GB
- classes: 35, 36, 41, 42
- status: REGISTERED
- dates: Filed 2026-02-24; Expiry 2036-02-24
- url: /mark/gb/c3154106-158a-4756-b8f6-116bc310172f
- key_factors: Seven letters against seven, differing only at the fourth character (C for Q, both sounding /k/) and the final vowel: VEN-KOR-a against VEN-KOR-ee. A live vertical-market software group, registered in the UK in classes 35, 36, 41 and 42 in May 2026 with a matching EU application filed February 2026 still pending. Two of those are the client's core classes. This changes the advice because it puts UK and EU filing sequencing, the wording of the class 42 specification, and whether to seek consent on the table before anything is filed. Mitigants: no class 9 cover, and an enterprise-software customer base is a real dissimilarity argument.
- verify: yes
- resolution: adversarial / Moderate — see finding #2

## VENCORE
- source_layer: Register
- owner: Symetri AB
- owner_country: SE
- classes: 42
- status: REGISTERED
- dates: Filed —; Expiry 2036-05-25
- url: /mark/wo/9a9d3dd9-5014-4ce1-b2bd-5a73c93cfc13
- key_factors: A registered international registration in class 42 — the client's core service class — for a near-homophone of the mark, held by a live engineering-software business, with EU and US applications in flight behind it. The international registration is the point: it can be extended into Japan and Korea by subsequent designation, which is precisely the reach mechanism that justified searching beyond the citation core. It argues for filing Japan and Korea early and for putting this registration on watch, and the US leg adds a live examination-citation risk. Mitigants: class 42 only, a distant B2B audience, and the term has demonstrably been shared before.
- verify: yes
- resolution: adversarial / Manageable — see finding #4

## VenQ
- source_layer: Register
- owner: LRG CONSULTANTS LIMITED
- owner_country: GB
- classes: 9, 16, 35, 41, 42
- status: REGISTERED
- dates: Filed 2025-07-28; Expiry 2035-07-28
- url: /mark/gb/8cbb976f-cfe1-4cf1-bbd7-4d3d1a211b28
- key_factors: The only third-party right in the entire search covering all three of the client's core classes (9, 41 and 42) in a reach market, under a mark that is the whole opening of VENQORI — registered in the UK in October 2025 to a live consultancy trading under it. The argument against treating this as decision-changing is real and was weighed: VENQ is short, and the -ORI tail carries more than half the mark's length and all of its rhythm. It is promoted anyway because a registered UK right spanning 9, 41 and 42 held by an active operator is what turns a routine UK filing into a choice — whether to file at all, whether to accept notification and opposition risk, or whether to approach the owner. Mitigant: registered only in October 2025, so the five-year non-use clock has barely started and the breadth of that specification is unlikely to be matched by real use.
- verify: yes
- resolution: adversarial / Manageable — see finding #8

## qori
- source_layer: Register
- owner: Firepay Merchant Services
- owner_country: FR
- classes: 9, 35, 36, 42
- status: REGISTERED
- dates: Filed 2021-10-12; Expiry 2031-10-12
- url: /mark/em/539c21aa-9d8f-438b-b140-23a16d4b4660
- key_factors: An EU registration of the bare word QORI in classes 9 and 42 — the client's two principal classes — live to 2031. VENQORI wholly contains QORI, and QORI is the visually unusual half of the mark. The counter-argument was weighed and is genuine: VENQORI is a unitary coined word, VEN has no independent meaning, and dissecting the mark is artificial. It is reported anyway because an earlier EU word mark wholly contained in the later mark in the same classes is a textbook opposition foundation, and the client cannot sensibly choose between an EU-wide filing and national filings without knowing it exists. Mitigants: registered April 2022, so the five-year non-use grace ends April 2027, and the owner is a payments business whose use is likely confined to that field — field divergence argues strongly against real-world confusion even where it does not answer an opposition.
- verify: yes
- resolution: adversarial / Manageable — see finding #6

## QORI
- source_layer: Register
- owner: InCert
- owner_country: LU
- classes: 9, 38, 42, 45
- status: PENDING
- dates: Filed 2026-08-11; Expiry 2036-08-11
- url: /mark/em/7a0fb9c1-6534-4467-8838-23a937da8e53
- key_factors: A second, unconnected EU proprietor of the bare word QORI in the client's classes 9 and 42, filed recently and still pending. Its independent significance is that it shows the element being actively taken up at the EU office now rather than sitting on a stale 2022 filing: two live EU claimants to the contained element, not one. That bears on the EU filing decision and on the client's own priority strategy, since an unexamined application's scope can still shift. Mitigants: pending and unexamined, so it may narrow or fail, and the owner's business is unverified on this record and should be confirmed before any approach.
- verify: yes
- resolution: adversarial / Manageable — see finding #7

## QORIA
- source_layer: Register
- owner: Qoria Holdings Pty Ltd
- owner_country: AU
- classes: 9, 41, 42
- status: REGISTERED
- dates: Filed —; Expiry 2033-09-28
- url: /mark/wo/f99ca2ce-9b6b-41ac-9318-90a66bfad14b
- key_factors: Of every owner in the search holding a QORI-formative mark, this one's customers come closest to the client's: an active digital-safety software business selling downloadable and cloud software to schools and to households. It holds all three of the client's core classes across the UK and the US and, critically, on an international registration that can be designated into Japan and Korea. The counter-reading is fair — QORIA and VENQORI as whole words are reasonably distinguishable and the VEN- onset dominates the first impression — but an active consumer-facing software brand with a real portfolio and a route into the citation core is a plausible and capable opponent, and the client's own international filing plan should be set with this registration in view. Mitigants: the field is child online safety, not water quality, and the difference in the opening syllable is a genuine point for the rating step. Note also that a second, unconnected French proprietor holds QORIA, which materially weakens this owner's claim to exclusivity in the element.
- verify: yes
- resolution: distinguished / Manageable — see finding #5
